- **sdk-api.availability**: 1. Load the symbol index for `--module` (extract + cache on first use). 2. Find every symbol whose qualified name matches exactly, or — failing an exact match — whose qualified name or title matches case-insensitively. All matches are returned (a symbol can have several declarations). 3. Project each match to `SymbolOut` and emit. The macOS availability lives in each match's `availability` field. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-api/0001-symbol-queries/commands/sdk-api.availability.md))
- **sdk-api.check**: 1. Load the symbol index for `--module` (extract + cache on first use). 2. Look up `&lt;symbol&gt;` by exact qualified name. When several declarations share the name (overloads, cross-extension redeclarations), the **first** match is reported — this is an existence check, not an enumeration. 3. Project the matched symbol (if any) to the `SymbolOut` shape and emit. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-api/0001-symbol-queries/commands/sdk-api.check.md))
- **sdk-api.enums**: 1. Load the symbol index for `--module` (extract + cache on first use). 2. Collect the members of `&lt;type&gt;` and keep only those whose kind is an enum case (`swift.enum.case`). 3. Project each case to `SymbolOut` and emit. A type with no enum cases yields an empty `cases` array, not an error. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-api/0001-symbol-queries/commands/sdk-api.enums.md))
- **sdk-api.members**: 1. Load the symbol index for `--module` (extract + cache on first use). 2. Resolve `&lt;type&gt;` to its container symbol and collect every symbol related to it by `memberOf`, sorted by member title. 3. Project each member to `SymbolOut` and emit. An unknown type yields an empty `members` array, not an error. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-api/0001-symbol-queries/commands/sdk-api.members.md))
- **sdk-api.search**: 1. Load the symbol index for `--module` (extract + cache on first use). 2. Rank symbols against the lowercased query by a fixed precedence — exact title &gt; title prefix &gt; qualified-name prefix &gt; title contains &gt; qualified-name contains — breaking ties by title, and take the first `--limit`. 3. Project each result to `SymbolOut` and emit. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-api/0001-symbol-queries/commands/sdk-api.search.md))

- **sdk-search.get**: 1. Load the embedded corpus. 2. For each id in order, look it up. Found patterns are projected to `PatternOutput`; unknown ids are collected as missing. 3. Emit the found patterns: a **single object** when exactly one id was found, otherwise a **JSON array** of pattern objects. 4. If any id was missing, write a notice to stderr and exit 1 — the found patterns are still printed to stdout first. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-search/0001-pattern-search/commands/sdk-search.get.md))
- **sdk-search.list**: 1. Load the embedded corpus. 2. Walk the patterns in corpus order, grouping by `category`. With `--category`, keep only patterns whose category matches case-insensitively. 3. Emit groups in the order each category first appears in the corpus; within a group, patterns keep corpus order. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-search/0001-pattern-search/commands/sdk-search.list.md))
- **sdk-search.search**: 1. Load the embedded corpus and build the BM25 search engine. 2. Reject an empty query (no positional args) with a `ValidationError`. 3. **Single-query mode** (exactly one positional arg, including a single quoted multi-word string): run the search and emit a `SearchOutput`. 4. **Batch mode** (two or more positional args, each a distinct query): run each query in order and emit a `BatchSearchOutpu ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/sdk-search/0001-pattern-search/commands/sdk-search.search.md))

- **sdp-be-dto**: Use when you need request or response payload contracts at a backend boundary, when a payload reaches a use case unvalidated, or when an internal entity leaks out the API ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-be-dto.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-be-entity**: Use when a domain concept needs a typed object with identity and self-validation ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-be-entity.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-be-handler**: Use when adding or changing an HTTP endpoint that exposes a backend use case, when a request reaches the use case without being validated, or when business logic is leaking into the transport layer ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-be-handler.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-be-migration**: Use when adding or altering a database table, column, index, or constraint, or when the schema needs to evolve safely alongside a new entity or feature ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-be-migration.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-be-repository**: Use when adding or changing how an entity is read from or written to the database, when a use case talks to Drizzle directly, or when raw rows or ORM types leak ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-be-repository.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-be-router**: Use when you need to expose REST endpoints for a resource, wire HTTP methods to handlers, or group routes under a path prefix in a backend ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-be-router.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-be-service**: Use when domain business logic spans multiple entities or repositories, when rules or transactions are leaking into a handler or use case, or when persistence and HTTP concerns are mixed into one class ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-be-service.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-be-usecase**: Use when a backend operation spans services/repos, handler logic grows fat, or a transaction needs one owner ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-be-usecase.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-checklist**: Use when a feature spec exists and needs requirement-quality audit before implementation. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-checklist.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-clarify**: Use when a feature spec has vague adjectives, open decisions or TODO markers and you need to resolve them before planning, or when acceptance criteria are not yet testable. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-clarify.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-constitution**: Use when creating, amending, ratifying, or versioning the project constitution, or when project principles drift from the inherited organizational law. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-constitution.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-debug**: Use when a test fails, an error appears during implementation, or a bug resists the first fix attempt ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-debug.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-design-to-react**: Use when you have a design screenshot, Figma/Sketch/XD export, or wireframe and need the matching React component built ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-fe-design-to-react.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-expo-api-route**: Use when adding a server-side endpoint inside an Expo Router app, when a BFF route is missing validation or auth, or when business logic leaks into an API route handler ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-fe-expo-api-route.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-expo-native-module**: Use when you need to access a device API or native platform capability that the Expo SDK does not cover, or when a JS-only implementation is too slow and the work must run in Swift/Kotlin. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-fe-expo-native-module.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-expo-screen**: Use when adding a route under app/ in an Expo Router app, when a screen renders blank while loading, when a data error is swallowed silently, or when logic leaks into app/ ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-fe-expo-screen.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-filter**: Use when you need a filter bar that lets users search and narrow down a list or data table by one or more fields ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-fe-filter.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-form**: Use when a screen needs to collect user input for an entity. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-fe-form.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-page**: Use when you need to scaffold a new routed view for a feature ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-fe-page.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-service**: Use when a component is calling fetch or axios directly, when API URLs and request shapes are scattered across pages, or when you need a typed client layer for an API resource ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-fe-service.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-site-identity**: Use when you need to match an existing brand from a live site URL or screenshots, redesign while keeping its look, or audit visual consistency across SDP projects. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-fe-site-identity.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-fe-table**: Use when you need to display a collection of records with pagination, sorting and per-row actions in a page. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-fe-table.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-finish**: Use quando implementacao e revisao estiverem concluidas e a branch de feature precisar ser finalizada. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-finish.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-implement**: Use when you have a complete tasks.md plan and an approved plan.md ready for implementation. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-implement.md) · 2 copies)

- **sdp-mr-template**: sdp-mr-template ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-mr-template.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-plan**: Use when a feature spec is approved and planning artifacts are missing before implementation begins. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-plan.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-qa**: Use when you need to verify that the implementation actually works, when you need an evidence-based quality score before review, or when automated checks have not been run after recent changes ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-qa.md) · 2 copies)
- **sdp-review**: Use when an implementation phase or task is complete and needs spec compliance and code quality review before merging or closing. ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-review.md) · 2 copies)

The chapter The engine controls, under spec/design_output/config.md, says what each value means. - **se-agent-control-wanted-full**: agent control / wanted: sets ask.wanted to full. What the owner wants said at the end of this turn. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-agent-control-wanted-full.md))
- **se-agent-control-wanted-quiet**: agent control / wanted: sets ask.wanted to quiet. What the owner wants said at the end of this turn. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-agent-control-wanted-quiet.md))

- **se-config-answer-ceiling**: config / answer / ceiling: sets answer.ceiling to what you type. The score where the gate names the band rewrite in its log line. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-answer-ceiling.md))
- **se-config-answer-enabled-false**: config / answer / enabled: sets answer.enabled to false. The door holding the owner's prompt first. false takes it out. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-answer-enabled-false.md))
- **se-config-answer-enabled-true**: config / answer / enabled: sets answer.enabled to true. The door holding the owner's prompt first. false takes it out. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-answer-enabled-true.md))
- **se-config-answer-warnAt**: config / answer / warnAt: sets answer.warnAt to what you type. The score where the gate starts writing a warn line to the log. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-answer-warnAt.md))
- **se-config-answer-words**: config / answer / words: sets answer.words to what you type. The words an answer holds outside its code and tables. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-answer-words.md))
- **se-config-ask-wanted-full**: config / ask / wanted: sets ask.wanted to full. What the owner wants said at the end of this turn. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-ask-wanted-full.md))
- **se-config-ask-wanted-quiet**: config / ask / wanted: sets ask.wanted to quiet. What the owner wants said at the end of this turn. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-ask-wanted-quiet.md))
- **se-config-ask-wanted-short**: config / ask / wanted: sets ask.wanted to short. What the owner wants said at the end of this turn. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-ask-wanted-short.md))
- **se-config-battery-budget**: config / battery / budget: sets battery.budget to what you type. The time the whole check takes at most. Past it, the check prints a warning naming its slowest part and its slowest cases. 0 switches it off. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-battery-budget.md))
- **se-config-battery-runs**: config / battery / runs: sets battery.runs to what you type. The check's runs the stamp keeps at one commit. A retro reads each part's median over them. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-battery-runs.md))
- **se-config-budget-handBack**: config / budget / handBack: sets budget.handBack to what you type. The median time a hand-back takes to land over a group of many tickets, with about ten times headroom over a box's measure, and 10 where that stays under a millisecond. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-budget-handBack.md))
- **se-config-budget-pull**: config / budget / pull: sets budget.pull to what you type. The median time the pull takes to hand out a leaf over a group of many tickets, with about ten times headroom over a box's measure, and 10 where that stays under a millisecond. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-budget-pull.md))
- **se-config-budget-resolver**: config / budget / resolver: sets budget.resolver to what you type. The median time the guidance resolver takes over many notes, with about ten times headroom over a box's measure, and 10 where that stays under a millisecond. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-budget-resolver.md))
- **se-config-budget-stale**: config / budget / stale: sets budget.stale to what you type. The median time the query for stale steps takes to read, hash and compare the notes a hold names, with about ten times headroom over a box's measure, and 10 where that stays under a millisecond. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-budget-stale.md))
- **se-config-cloud-fleetRoutine**: config / cloud / fleetRoutine: sets cloud.fleetRoutine to what you type. The id of the stored routine that checks the fleet, which ./RUNME.sh cloud trigger names. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-cloud-fleetRoutine.md))
- **se-config-code-fileLines**: config / code / fileLines: sets code.fileLines to what you type. The lines a file holds at most. The write door refuses a write that grows past it. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-code-fileLines.md))
- **se-config-code-functionLines**: config / code / functionLines: sets code.functionLines to what you type. The lines a function holds at most. The write door refuses a write that grows past it. - **se-config-context-handoverAt**: config / context / handoverAt: sets context.handoverAt to what you type. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-context-handoverAt.md))

- **se-config-fleet-idleAfter**: config / fleet / idleAfter: sets fleet.idleAfter to what you type. The age of a held box's tip past which ./RUNME.sh cloud fleet raises an idle wake. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-fleet-idleAfter.md))
- **se-config-grace-finish**: config / grace / finish: sets grace.finish to what you type. The calls the finish hold lets pass before it stops hard. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-grace-finish.md))

- **se-config-ops-keepDone**: config / ops / keepDone: sets ops.keepDone to what you type. The span a done or cancelled operation stays under ops. Zero keeps it. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-ops-keepDone.md))
- **se-config-ops-keepFailed**: config / ops / keepFailed: sets ops.keepFailed to what you type. The span a failed operation stays under ops. Zero keeps it. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-ops-keepFailed.md))
- **se-config-plan-everyCalls**: config / plan / everyCalls: sets plan.everyCalls to what you type. The calls between two asks of the engine's three questions. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-plan-everyCalls.md))
- **se-config-plan-grace**: config / plan / grace: sets plan.grace to what you type. The calls that pass after the ask, before every call waits on the plan's answer. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-plan-grace.md))
- **se-config-plan-mostOpen**: config / plan / mostOpen: sets plan.mostOpen to what you type. The todos the plan holds open, past which the third question stays away. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-plan-mostOpen.md))
- **se-config-processes-placements**: config / processes / placements: sets processes.placements to what you type. the lists of module instances that share one process, where each instance in no list takes a process of its own ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-processes-placements.md))
- **se-config-pull-cap**: config / pull / cap: sets pull.cap to what you type. The size up to which one tool answer reaches the model whole. Past it, the client hands back a preview. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-pull-cap.md))
- **se-config-pull-margin**: config / pull / margin: sets pull.margin to what you type. The room the pull keeps free below the cap. A hand-out past cap less margin splits, and the next pull on the step prints the rest. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-pull-margin.md))
- **se-config-queue-block**: config / queue / block: sets queue.block to what you type. the score a ticket takes for each ticket its chain holds up ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-queue-block.md))
- **se-config-queue-day**: config / queue / day: sets queue.day to what you type. the score a ticket takes for each whole day it stands ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-queue-day.md))
- **se-config-queue-fail**: config / queue / fail: sets queue.fail to what you type. the score a ticket takes for each hand-back that failed on it ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-queue-fail.md))
- **se-config-restated-pointer**: config / restated / pointer: sets restated.pointer to what you type. The run a heading shares with the heading its pointer names before the rule draws. - **se-config-restated-rule**: config / restated / rule: sets restated.rule to what you type. The run two rule lines of two guidance notes share before the rule draws. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-restated-rule.md))
- **se-config-stop-enabled-false**: config / stop / enabled: sets stop.enabled to false. The tooth. false ends every turn where the agent asks to end it. ([mb-89/quackitect](https://github.com/mb-89/quackitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/se-config-stop-enabled-false.md))
